# Thread Commit Timing Report

Generated: 2026-06-07 09:14 +03:00
Baseline ref: report branch `HEAD` at `169bc9a67` (`169bc9a6770f7ab15286037d9215cbdd912503bf`)
Project start: 2026-05-12 16:25 +03:00 at `942d29309` - Phase 2 Task 1: extend CellStyle with font/fill/border/alignment and add StyleRegistry to Workbook
History scope: first-parent history from project start through the baseline ref.

## Method

- A thread is represented by each first-parent merge commit.
- Thread identity is best-effort: exact second-parent branch ref when still available, otherwise a branch name parsed from the merge subject, otherwise the merge subject without the `Merge` prefix.
- Introduced commits are `first_parent..second_parent` for that merge, excluding commits already reachable from the first parent at the merge point.
- Discovery time is the timestamp of the first introduced commit. This is a Git-history proxy for when the thread first produced durable work, not the chat/session creation time.
- Implementation span is first introduced commit to last introduced commit. Integration lag is last introduced commit to the first-parent merge commit.
- Direct first-parent commits are summarized separately because they do not have a merge-thread identity.
